### Trello board snapshot (open cards by list)

**User Interaction**
- Chat to negotiate event details — ## User Story  As an event suggester, to take the stress of organising away/if I’m unsure exactly where and when my friends would like to meet, I want a way for the whole group to pitch-in and discuss the details of the meetup.  ## Evidence  > "multiple dates is probably the basis, but multiple times at least, right? So for example, if someone wanted to go at 3pm as opposed to 4pm, then they could type it below in some comments or forum segment beneath, and then you could obviously suggest it"  **Luca Morgan**
- Make meetups for multiple groups — ## User Story  As a suggester, I would like to be able to make a meetup for multiple friend groups without having to create a new group combining others.  ## Evidence  > "Can I combine groups guys, say I want to add these two groups people" or "I know people who've also had parties and they've invited multiple friend groups... like Anshu's birthday party, he invited his computing friend group and he also invited his Westminster friend group. So it's two distinct groups, but he combined both."  **Zack Foreman**
- Options for suggesting a meetup confusing at first — ## User Story  As a suggester, I’m not sure at first how to use the three ways of suggesting a meetup - ‘float it’, ‘flexible’ and ‘set it'. I would need to use each of these features a few times before I know which to use for the type of meetup I want to suggest.  ## Evidence  > “Umm I’m not sure really whether to use float it or flexible for a meetup I’m where I’m not sure about the exact details. I think I’d need to use each option a few times before I can know which to use.”  **Luca Morgan**
- Organiser controls whether friends can propose their own times — ## User Story  As on organiser, I would like to control whether or not my friends can propose alternative times for the meetup when the meetup must happen at a set time, take a football match for example.  ## Evidence  > "I think there should be an option for a user to have just a single proposed time, but also have that stage where users, their friends can add, like, can propose their own times. So, like, maybe something like it's default, but that you can propose more times, but on concrete events, like maybe there's a checkbox or something a switch that you can hit which is like this is a concrete event you cannot change it."  **Tom Carvell**

**User Interface**
- Anonymous suggestions — ## User Story  As an organiser, when I make a suggestion that a group meets up, I would like to clearly know that this suggestion is anonymous.  ## Evidence  > "I think people always are really self-conscious of being the one to push things. And nobody likes being the one that is like making people commit to ideas or chasing people up or requiring that people make decisions. And so by being able to make it like an anonymous thing, there's no pressure on that person at all to have any sort of involvement."  **Tom Carvell**  ## Feedback  > “This is good. It’s now clear to me that no one will know it was me who suggested the meetup.”
- Float option should not be a different shade — ## User Story  It’s unintuitive when I look to create a new meetup to see the “Float” option as a different colour to the “flexible” and “fixed” options.  ## Evidence  > "I'm not sure why Float is a slightly different shade, but it's eye-catching."  **Luca Morgan**
- Searching for friends to add to a group — ## User Story  As a user, if I have a lot of friends and want to make a new group I don’t want to scroll through a large list of users to find a friend because that might take a long time and I might miss them.  ## Evidence  > "I'm not seeing a way to search for names in the group... I feel like that would probably be quite useful."  **Luca Morgan**

**Closed**
- Recurring events — ## User Story  As a suggester and an attendant, I would like to be able to have an event that is recurring, a pub quiz for example, so i don’t need to make the same event each week myself. Or if not a recurring event, I’d like to be able to create a new event from an existing template say to speed up the process of making the new meetup when I do so often.  ## Evidence  > "I think certain events... things particularly like D&D tend to be recurring. They tend to be around the same time each week or each couple of weeks, right? And it's not unique to that specifically... it's a very common thing, I think, where people play board games on a certain night, or they tend to go to the pub at a certain time."  **Luca Morgan**  ## Feedback  > “Oh yeah, this is much more convienient. Now I don’t have to type stuff for no reason and repeat myself.”
- Creating a meetup page is overwhelming — ## User Story  As an organiser, when I create a new meetup there is lots of information on that page and it can feel slightly overwhelming.  ## Evidence  > "it takes me a second to read through to make sure that everything I understand, like where to put every single thing and what each thing does. This is like, especially with the boxes. So maybe adding more space between the boxes might help or I don't know, laying it out a bit differently."  **Zack Foreman**  ## Feedback  > "As a general wizard, it's pretty cool. It's very simple... it's easy to fill out. It's very clear as well. I think as soon as I read these, I also kind of almost immediately figured out what each one is like."  **Zack Foreman**
- Suggest multiple times for a meetup — ## User Story  As an event organiser, I would like to suggest multiple times for a meetup in case my friends are busy and might not make the first time.  ## Evidence  > “maybe even the ability to put multiple dates and have a voting system or something”  **Luca Morgan**  ## Feedback  > "And then for this, you can vote on like either of these. Oh, and then you can also add a time. Okay. Can anyone add a time for this one? Yes, I can add a time. Okay, cool." > > "That's actually pretty elegant."  **Luca Morgan**
- Date & time selection boxes — ## User Story  As an event suggester, it would be easier to select a date and time from a calendar dropdown rather than enter a time in a strict format.  ## Evidence  > "Why are we doing year, month, day? I mean, if you want, I guess. It would be kind of nice to have like, I don't know what extent, but like a calendar drop down or something would also be kind of nice for this sort of thing."  **Luca Morgan**  > "If it has to be formatted like that, it would probably be nicer to just have some sort of drop-down, like a scroll, kind of like the clock app, or timing stopwatch app, sorry, and phones, if you get what I mean."  **Luca Morgan**  ## Feedback  > _“Ah, you did do the drop-down calendar. That's pretty cool."_ > > _"Yes, you did the scroll as well. Nice."_  **Luca Morgan**
- Select meetup times relative to now — ## User Story  As an organiser, I would like to select the time of a meetup relative to now because that would be simpler than picking the time from a calendar which shows me many more options than I will pick from.  ## Evidence  > "It feels like if I said in two days time or Wednesday this week would be much more convenient than having to type out the date entirely every time."  **Tom Carvell**  ## Feedback  > "Oh that's much easier, like I don't have to think about what the actual date is, I just know I want to do something this weekend."  **Tom Carvell**
- Floating a meetup — ## User Story  As an meetup suggester I would like to be able to suggest a meetup more loosely - just float the idea and have the group decide when and where to meet more collaboratively.  ## Evidence  > "I think that's part of the issue with this is when you do the suggested meetup, like I have to put in a hard time."  **Felicity Turner**  ## Feedback  > "That actually does seem pretty cool. It's a quick way to decide to meet up."  **Luca Morgan**
- Suggest a single time and many times on a single page — ## User Story  As an organiser, it makes more sense to have a page that defaults to one time and allows me to add more times when I’m picking a time rather than two separate pages.  ## Evidence  > "I have a suggestion. Why what's the point of these being separate? Why don't we just have so like here we have added time, why don't we have the UI so that when you go onto this page there's just one option with an add a time below it. So by default is only one but we can remove it all the way down to any that being one option."  **Tom Carvell**  ## Feedback  > “Oh this makes much more sense, like before I wasn't sure whether to use this one or that one but now it's just obvious, like you start with one time and add more if you need to."  **Tom Carvell**
- Resolve meetup time by voting — ## User Story  As a meetup participant, when the meetup has multiple possible times put forward, it would be best if the time with the most votes is chosen.  ## Evidence  > “Umm yeah I think it would make sense for the final time to be decided by a vote, like a whatsapp poll or something, yeah.”  **Tom Carvell**  ## Feedback  > “Oh ok yeah this is kind of what I was thinking of. I like how the options go dark when I select them so I know which ones I’ve put myself forward for and I can also clearly see all the options which is nice.”  **Tom Carvell**

**User Need**
- Make joining meetups simple — ## User Story  As a friend, I don't want to spend time convincing my friends to get BeThere, I just want us to meet-up.  ## Evidence  > "blind or raw suggesting it onto a group chat and then just hoping people will download it is probably not it, just doesn't feel that reasonable."  **Fangyi Lin**  > “I feel like in... I feel like some of my friendship groups probably would tend to be perfectly reasonable and like, ‘Fight to do this and I'll download it.’ Because it really is like a zero-cost thing, to be honest. And then some would probably be a bit more hesitant, purely because they're just like, Can't be bothered.”  **Luca Morgan**  ## Feedback  > "It's just a link and then you click on it and it immediately brings you to a web browser. That does feel much more seamless, I will say actually."  **Tom Carvell**  > "I feel like the alternative to that would have been, hey guys, let's download an app and then when you're on the app, put in this link and then we can plan something. Whereas I feel like with this, it's much more like... they will probably click on it and it will probably be like, oh okay, sure."  **Will Groves**  > "it makes it get introduced in a much more purposeful manner, whereas instead of just kind of like vaguely trying to suggest it would be a good idea to use this app, it's much more like, we are using this app now, here's the meetup that we're planning. Yeah, I could definitely see it going better for sure, actually."  **Fangyi Lin**
- Multi-party meet-ups — ## User Story  As a student in numerous friend groups, I want to suggest meet-ups bringing together several different groups of people, and I want friends to be able to bring along their friends.  ## Evidence  > "a lot of the time you might want to invite several groups of people, like as in you're bringing like two friend groups together, but you probably wouldn't want to create like a new group of all of them, because that just seems like a bit of extra effort. Or even like just people who aren't in a group in general, and you're just kind of like, this is a friend of a friend group. let's have them as like a plus one to join my friend and so on and so forth. But you don't really want to invite them to like the group because you don't really want to invite them all the time."  **Luca Morgan**  ## Feedback  > "Wait, can I plus-one my flatmate to this? [does it] Oh that was easy."  **Thomas Gonzalez**  > "Okay so it shows everyone that he's been added and who invited him — yeah that's good, no one's getting surprised on the day."  **Luca Morgan**
